Algorithm for pair descent:
- Choose pair sequence. Could cycle p=0..n-2 q=p+1 (adjacent). Or random pairs. Which is better? Adjacent mass transfers local; may reduce high local overlaps. Random pairs can reshuffle. We can maintain active lag info to target.
- Since pair update can transfer mass arbitrarily between p and q, including reverse, it is like moving mass along distance q-p. It can reduce overlap counts at that lag. It resembles 2-swap for binary. Starting from fractional, it can move mass.
- We can do multiple passes: for p in range(0,n-1): pair(p,p+1), then random pairs maybe using rng. Evaluate C after each; keep best c. If accept, update h,c. Passes until deadline.

But building D slices and Python overhead maybe more. We can adapt: stop if no improvement many times.

def add_first(D0,D1,r,other,h,xp,sign):
    # sign +1 for r=p, -1 for r=q
    # j ranges: [0,r-1], [r+1,n-1], remove other if within
    for js,je in segments(...):
       if js>je continue
       m0 = n - 1 + js - r
       # length = je-js+1
       D1[m0:m0+len] += sign * (1 - h[js:je+1])
       D0[m0:m0+len] += -xp * sign * (1 - h[js:je+1])  # because D1 increment = sign*coeff, D0=-xp*D1_increment
But if sign=-1, D1 decrement, D0 increment positive? Yes.

def pair_cd(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,max_iter,rng,use_targeted=True):
    h=h.copy(); c=c.copy(); cmax=c.max()
    L=2*n-1; center=n-1
    best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    stagnation=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # choose p,q
       if mode random...:
          ...
       improved = pair_update(h,n,c,center,p,q,alpha grid,... cmax)
       if improved:
          stagnation=0
          if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
              best update
       else stagnation++
       if stagnation > threshold: choose restart? maybe random pairs
    return best_h,best_c,best_cmax
```

Let's implement pair_update code in detail.

```
def pair_update(h, n, c, cmax, p, q, grid1=256, grid2=800, tol=1e-10):
    if p == q: return False
    xp = h[p]; yq = h[q]; s = xp + yq
    a = max(0.0, s - 1.0); b = min(1.0, s)
    if b - a < 1e-14: return False
    L=2*n-1
    D0 = np.zeros(L); D1 = np.zeros(L); D2 = np.zeros(L); touched = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
    center=n-1
    # diag
    D0[center] += -xp*(1.0-xp); D1[center]+=1.0; D2[center]-=1.0
    D0[center] += s*(1.0-s) - yq*(1.0-yq); D1[center]+=2.0*s-1.0; D2[center]-=1.0
    touched[center]=True
    # cross if q-p in bounds (always)
    m1 = center + (q - p)
    old1 = yq*(1.0-xp)
    D0[m1] += s - old1; D1[m1] -= s + 1.0; D2[m1] += 1.0; touched[m1]=True
    m2 = center + (p - q)
    old2 = xp*(1.0-yq)
    D0[m2] += -old2; D1[m2] += 1.0 - s; D2[m2] += 1.0; touched[m2]=True
    # add first/second with segments
```
Segments helper to avoid duplication:
```
def add_slice(D1,D0, m_start, m_len, values, sign, xp):
   if m_len<=0: return
   inc = sign * values
   D1[m_start:m_start+m_len] += inc
   D0[m_start:m_start+m_len] += -xp*inc
   touched[...]=True
```
But touched global? We can inside pair_update use local and set.
Need values aligned ascending m.
For first r:
Segment j from js to je (ascending). m_start = center + js - r; length = je-js+1; values=1-h[js:je+1]; sign = +1 for r=p, -1 for r=q. Add.
But if js=r+1 to r? no.
Segments for first r excluding other:
- js=0, je=r-1 (if r>0 and other not in)
- js=r+1, je=n-1
Split if other in [0,r-1] or [r+1,n-1]. We can write a small function `segments_all_except(r, other)` returning list. Since n small, implement:
```
segs=[]
if r > 0 and other < r:
   if other>0: segs.append((0,other-1))
   segs.append((other+1,r-1))

`project_feasible` bisection:
```
def project_feasible(z,n):
 target=n/2.0; z=np.asarray(z,float).copy()
 lo=z.min()-1.0; hi=z.max()+1.0
 # if after clip hi? sum at lo should >=target, at hi <=target
 for _ in range(60):
   th=(lo+hi)/2
   s=np.clip(z-th,0,1).sum()
   if s>target: lo=th
   else: hi=th
 th=(lo+hi)/2
 return np.clip(z-th,0,1)
```

The local evaluation function:
```
def evaluate_pair_candidates(h,n,c,mask,D0,D1,D2,cm, max_unaff, xs, max_c):
   mat = cm[None,:] + D0m[None,:] + D1m[None,:]*xs[:,None] + D2m[None,:]*xs[:,None]**2
   f = mat.max(axis=1)
   np.maximum(f, max_unaff, out=f)
   return f
```
But repeated code. We can inline for coarse and refine to avoid closure. Use top-level helper? The rules: no closures/lambdas; helper top-level okay. Could define `pair_eval_f(mask, c, D0,D1,D2, max_unaff, xs)` top-level. But need pass arrays. Fine.
```
def pair_eval_f(c, D0, D1, D2, mask, max_unaff, xs):
    cm = c[mask]; D0m=D0[mask]; ...
    x = xs.reshape(-1,1); x2=x*x
    vals = cm[None,:] + D0m[None,:] + D1m[None,:]*x + D2m[None,:]*x2
    f = vals.max(axis=1)
    np.maximum(f, max_unaff, out=f)
    return f
```

Pseudo pair_cd:
```
cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_cmax=best_cmax
stall=0
while time<deadline:
  if stall > 5000: # no improvement many
       # perturb cur from best with small noise
       sigma = 0.02 * max(1, stall/10000)
       cur_h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n),n)
       cur_c,cur_cmax = compute_c(cur_h,n)
       if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.02: # too bad, shrink noise?
       keep cur anyway? Pair from perturbed may recover.
       stall=0
  else choose pair and update; if improvement: if cur_cmax < best_cmax: update best; stall=0; else stall++
  # if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.005 for long, maybe reset to best
```

Let's implement `pair_cd(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,rng)` where deadline is when to stop. It returns best h,c,cmax found from that start. It maintains current as starting point (not best) and best. It uses stochastic selection. If it stalls, it resets current to best (or slightly perturbed) to escape. Let's design robust:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,rng):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=c.copy(); cur_cmax=cmax
    idx=np.arange(n); L=2*n-1; center=n-1
    state=0; it=0; stall=0; active=[]; last_active=-1
    pert_level=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if it % 500 == 0: recompute cur_c? maybe if drift.
       if stall > 1500:
           # perturb current from best
           sigma = 0.02 + 0.005*(pert_level%5)
           cur_h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n), n)
           cur_c, cur_cmax = compute_c(cur_h,n)
           if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.01:
              # too large, use smaller sigma or just set to best
              cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_cmax=best_cmax
           pert_level +=1
           stall=0
           active=[]
       # choose pair as above
       ok,new_cmax=pair_update(...)
       if ok:
           cur_cmax=new_cmax
           if cur_cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
               best_cmax=cur_cmax; best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_c=cur_c.copy()
           stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
       it+=1
       # periodically recompute cur_c to avoid drift, and if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.005 reset to best?
       if it % 1000 ==0:
           cur_c, cur_cmax = compute_c(cur_h,n)
           if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.001:
               # maybe current drifted not improving; reset to best to focus
               cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_cmax=best_cmax
               active=[]
    return best_h,best_c,best_cmax
```
But resetting to best when current worse may prevent escape if perturbation initially worse but can improve. Pair updates only accept improvements from current, so if perturbed C worse, it can still improve downward. We should not reset if slightly worse unless too bad. Maybe allow current C up to best+0.02. If exceeded, reset.
Stall perturbation: Use current best plus noise; if C worse by <0.02 keep; else reset to best.

```
def smooth_obj_grad(h, alpha, penalty):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1; dx=2.0/n; target=n/2.0
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m)
    s=np.exp(z).sum()
    w=np.exp(z)/s
    base = c - m? Actually f = dx*(m + np.log(s)/alpha) # since sum exp(z)=s; logsumexp(alpha c)=alpha*m+log(s)
    val = dx*(m + np.log(s)/alpha) + 0.5*penalty*(h.sum()-target)**2
    # grad
    if fftconvolve is not None:
       W1=fftconvolve(1.0-h, w, 'full')[n-1:n+n-1]
       W2=fftconvolve(h, w[::-1], 'full')[n-1:n+n-1]
    else:
       W1=np.convolve(1.0-h, w, 'full')[n-1:n+n-1]
       W2=np.convolve(h, w[::-1], 'full')[n-1:n+n-1]
    grad = dx*(W1 - W2) + penalty*(h.sum()-target)*np.ones(n)
    return val, grad
```
Need ensure `w` length L. `fftconvolve` with real returns length n+L-1 =3n-2; slice n-1 to 3n-2? `n-1:n+n-1` length n. Good.
But z shifted by max m. logsumexp(alpha c) = alpha*m + log(sum exp(alpha(c-m))). f = dx*(m + log(s)/alpha). Yes.
If penalty=0, gradient at feasible max approximate.

def pair_eval_idx(cm, D0m, D1m, D2m, max_unaff, xs):
    x=xs.reshape(-1,1); xt=x*x
    vals = cm[None,:] + D0m[None,:] + D1m[None,:]*x + D2m[None,:]*xt
    f=vals.max(axis=1)
    if max_unaff > -1e300: np.maximum(f, max_unaff, out=f)
    return f
```

def choose_target_pair(c,h,n,center,active_m,rng):
   m=active_m[rng.randint(len(active_m))]
   d=m-center
   if d==0:
      scores = h*(1-h)
      # choose weighted top: use rng.rand < maybe argmax
      # random among top 20 fractional
      top = np.argsort(scores)[-20:]
      p = top[rng.randint(len(top))]
      q = rng.randint(n)
      if q==p: q=(p+1)%n
      return p,q
   if d>0:
      contrib = h[d:]*(1.0-h[:-d])
      # choose weighted among top 20 to avoid same
      top = np.argsort(contrib)[-min(20,len(contrib)):]
      k = top[rng.randint(len(top))]
      return k, k+d
   else:
      D=-d
      contrib = h[:-D]*(1.0-h[D:])
      top = np.argsort(contrib)[-min(20,len(contrib)):]
      k=top[...]
      return D+k, k
```

```
def build_active_pairs(c,h,n,rng):
    center=n-1; L=2*n-1
    thresh_order = np.argsort(c)
    top_lags = thresh_order[-max(8,L//25):]
    pairs=[]
    for m in top_lags:
        d=m-center
        t=8
        if d==0:
            scores=h*(1-h)
            idxs=np.argsort(scores)[-t:]
            for p in idxs:
                pairs.append((p,(p+1)%n))
                pairs.append((p,(p+n//2)%n))
        elif d>0:
            contrib=h[d:]*(1-h[:-d])
            idxs=np.argsort(contrib)[-min(t,len(contrib)):]
            for k in idxs: pairs.append((int(k), int(k+d)))
        else:
            D=-d
            contrib=h[:-D]*(1-h[D:])
            idxs=np.argsort(contrib)[-min(t,len(contrib)):]
            for k in idxs: pairs.append((int(D+k), int(k)))
    # shuffle pairs
    if len(pairs)>0:
       perm=np.arange(len(pairs)); rng.shuffle(perm); pairs=[pairs[i] for i in perm]
    return pairs
```

Let's estimate lines. We can write L-BFGS compact:
```
def smooth_calc(h, alpha, penalty, need_val):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; target=n/2.0
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); s=np.exp(z).sum(); w=np.exp(z)/s
    val=None
    if need_val: val=dx*(m+np.log(s)/alpha)+0.5*penalty*(h.sum()-target)**2
    if fftconvolve is not None:
       W1=fftconvolve(1.0-h,w,'full')[n-1:3*n-2]
       W2=fftconvolve(h,w[::-1],'full')[n-1:3*n-2]
    else:
       W1=np.convolve(1.0-h,w,'full')[n-1:3*n-2]
       W2=np.convolve(h,w[::-1],'full')[n-1:3*n-2]
    grad=dx*(W1-W2)+penalty*(h.sum()-target)
    return val, grad
```
`penalty*(sum-target)` broadcasts to grad (array+scalar). Good.
`lbfgs_f` can call smooth_calc. To avoid recomputing val in jac, we can define `lbfgs_f` and `lbfgs_jac`.

def lbfgs_opt(h,n,deadline):
    best_h=h.copy(); cbest=compute_c(best_h,n); cbest=cbest[1]*(2.0/n)
    b=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for alpha in (20.0,80.0,300.0,1000.0):
      for pen in (1e3,1e5):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        try:
           res=minimize(lbfgs_f,best_h,args=(alpha,pen),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=b,jac=lbfgs_jac,
                        options={'maxiter':150,'ftol':1e-12})
           nh=project_feasible(res.x,n); nc,cmax=compute_c(nh,n); C=cmax*2.0/n
           if C<cbest-1e-12: best_h,cbest=nh,C
        except Exception: pass
        if time.time()>deadline: break
    cmax=cbest*n/2.0? no cbest is C, need return h,c,cmax. c,_=compute_c...
    return best_h, *compute_c(best_h,n)
```
But `lbfgs_f` returns val; `lbfgs_jac` returns grad. Note `minimize` with `jac=lbfgs_jac` passes args to jac as (x, alpha, pen), okay.

`pair_cd` details. Need pair_update use current max_c unscaled. It returns (ok,newmax). We need update h/c in place. We'll write:
```
def pair_update(h,n,c,p,q,max_c,idx):
    if p==q: return False, max_c
    xp=h[p]; yq=h[q]; s=xp+yq
    a=max(0.0,s-1.0); b=min(1.0,s)
    if b-a < 1e-14: return False,max_c
    L=2*n-1; center=n-1
    D0=np.zeros(L); D1=np.zeros(L); D2=np.zeros(L); touched=np.zeros(L,bool)
    # diag
    D0[center] = -xp*(1-xp) + s*(1-s) - yq*(1-yq)
    D1[center] = 2.0*s
    D2[center] = -2.0
    touched[center]=True
    # cross
    m=center+q-p; old=yq*(1-xp)
    D0[m] += s-old; D1[m] += -(s+1.0); D2[m] += 1.0; touched[m]=True
    m=center+p-q; old=xp*(1-yq)
    D0[m] += -old; D1[m] += 1.0-s; D2[m] += 1.0; touched[m]=True
    # singles
    other=q
    for r, sign in ((p,1.0),(q,-1.0)):
        other = q if r==p else p
        keep=(idx!=r)&(idx!=other)
        j=idx[keep]
        if j.size:
            coeff=h[j]
            m=center+j-r
            D1[m] += -coeff
            D0[m] += xp*coeff
            touched[m]=True
            coeff=1.0-h[j]
            inc=sign*coeff
            m=center+r-j
            D1[m] += inc
            D0[m] += -xp*inc
            touched[m]=True
    mask=touched
    if not mask.any(): return False,max_c
    if mask.all(): max_unaff=-1e100
    else: max_unaff=float(c[~mask].max())
    im=mask.nonzero()[0]
    cm=c[im]; D0m=D0[im]; D1m=D1[im]; D2m=D2[im]
    xs=np.linspace(a,b,256)
    f=pair_eval_idx(cm,D0m,D1m,D2m,max_unaff,xs)
    i=int(f.argmin())
    if f[i] >= max_c - 1e-10: return False,max_c
    lo=xs[max(0,i-1)]; hi=xs[min(len(xs)-1,i+1)]
    for _ in range(3):
        g=400
        xs=np.linspace(lo,hi,g)
        f=pair_eval_idx(...)
        j=int(f.argmin())
        lo=xs[max(0,j-1)]; hi=xs[min(g-1,j+1)]
    x=float(xs[j]) # last loop j
    if x<a: x=a
    elif x>b: x=b
    newmax=float(np.max(c + D0 + D1*x + D2*x*x))
    if newmax < max_c - 1e-10:
        h[p]=x; h[q]=s-x
        c += D0 + D1*x + D2*x*x
        return True, newmax
    return False,max_c
```
Need initialize `j` before loop to avoid unbound if loop not? loop always runs 3. Use `best_x=xs[i]`.
After refine, `j` exists. Good.

Now, `build_active_pairs`: Use `rng` to shuffle. Need `np.argsort` on c each update; c length ~1400.
```
def build_active_pairs(c,h,n,rng):
    center=n-1; L=2*n-1
    order=np.argsort(c)
    tl=order[-max(8,L//20):].copy()
    if not np.any(tl==center): tl=np.append(tl, center)
    pairs=[]
    for m in tl:
       d=int(m)-center
       t=6
       if d==0:
          score=h*(1-h)
          idxs=np.argsort(score)[-t:]
          for p in idxs:
             p=int(p)
             pairs.append((p,(p+1)%n))
             pairs.append((p,(p+n//2)%n))
       elif d>0:
          if n-d <= 0: continue
          cont=h[d:]*(1-h[:-d])
          idxs=np.argsort(cont)[-min(t,len(cont)):]
          for k in idxs: pairs.append((int(k),int(k+d)))
       else:
          D=-d
          cont=h[:-D]*(1-h[D:])
          idxs=np.argsort(cont)[-min(t,len(cont)):]
          for k in idxs: pairs.append((int(D+k),int(k)))
    if len(pairs)>0:
       perm=np.arange(len(pairs)); rng.shuffle(perm); pairs=[pairs[int(k)] for k in perm]
    return pairs
```
Could have duplicate pairs; fine.
Maybe if `n//2 ==1`, q may equal p? if n=2 and (p+1)%n distinct, n//2=1 distinct. ok.

`pair_cd`:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,rng):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=c.copy(); cur_cmax=cmax
    idx=np.arange(n); L=2*n-1; center=n-1
    pairs=build_active_pairs(cur_c,cur_h,n,rng)
    state=0; it=0; stall=0; last=-999
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if it - last > 300 or len(pairs)==0:
           pairs=build_active_pairs(cur_c,cur_h,n,rng)
           last=it
       u=rng.random()
       if len(pairs)>0 and u<0.45:
           p,q=pairs[rng.randint(len(pairs))]
       elif u<0.75:
           p=state % (n-1); q=p+1; state+=1
       else:
           p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
           if q==p: q=(p+1)%n
       ok,newmax=pair_update(cur_h,n,cur_c,p,q,cur_cmax,idx)
       if ok:
           cur_cmax=newmax
           if cur_cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
               best_cmax=cur_cmax; best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_c=cur_c.copy()
           stall=0
       else:
           stall += 1
       it += 1
       if it % 1500 == 0:
           cur_c2,cur_cmax2=compute_c(cur_h,n)
           cur_c,cur_cmax=cur_c2,cur_cmax2
           if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.01:
               cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_cmax=best_cmax
               pairs=build_active_pairs(cur_c,cur_h,n,rng)
       if stall > max(2000, 4*n):
           sigma=0.015
           cur_h=project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n),n)
           cur_c,cur_cmax=compute_c(cur_h,n)
           if cur_cmax > best_cmax + 0.02:
               cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_cmax=best_cmax
           else:
               # keep perturbed; best inside should be perturbed? But best_c currently best of this run maybe lower than perturbed.
               # If perturbed worse, do not reset best. Pair updates can improve from perturbed, but if it doesn't, best remains.
               # If perturbed better, update best.
               if cur_cmax < best_cmax:
                   best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_c=cur_c.copy(); best_cmax=cur_cmax
           pairs=build_active_pairs(cur_c,cur_h,n,rng)
           stall=0
    return best_h,best_c,best_cmax
```
But if current after perturbation is worse, pair_update only accepts improvements from current; it may not reach best but can still find global? It starts from worse and descends; could find better than best. Good.
`best` initially start. If perturbed C > best, best remains previous. If perturbed C < best, update.

Code:
```
def lbfgs_opt(h,n,deadline):
    best_h=h.copy()
    _,best_max=compute_c(best_h,n)
    best_C=best_max*2.0/n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for alpha in (20.0,80.0,300.0,1000.0):
        for pen in (1e4,1e5):
            if time.time()>=deadline: break
            try:
                res=minimize(lbfgs_f,best_h,args=(alpha,pen),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=lbfgs_jac,
                             options={'maxiter':80,'ftol':1e-12})
                nh=project_feasible(res.x,n)
                _,nm=compute_c(nh,n)
                C=nm*2.0/n
                if C<best_C-1e-12:
                    best_C=C; best_h=nh
            except Exception:
                pass
            if time.time()>=deadline: break
    _,bm=compute_c(best_h,n)
    return best_h, bm  # wait need c array too. compute returns c,max.
```
Return `best_h, c, bm`.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
    except Exception:
        n=500; h=np.zeros(n); perm=rng.permutation(n); h[perm[:n//2]]=1.0
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c,cmax=compute_c(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_n=n; bestC=cmax*2.0/n
    if budget_s > 700:
       lb_deadline = min(deadline - 600, time.time()+120)
       if lb_deadline > time.time():
           try:
              h2,c2,cmax2=lbfgs_opt(best_h,best_n,lb_deadline)
              C2=cmax2*2.0/best_n
              if C2<bestC: best_h,best_c,best_n,bestC=h2,c2,best_n,C2
           except Exception: pass
    reserve = 180 if budget_s > 600 else 60
    main_deadline = deadline - reserve
    if main_deadline <= time.time(): main_deadline = time.time()+5
    best_h,best_c,bestn? pair_cd returns h,c,cmax;
```

def polish_adjacent(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,idx):

Let's add `polish_adjacent` compact; include before return if time >30. It may improve.

```
def polish_adjacent(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,idx):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=c.copy(); cur_cmax=cmax
    idx=np.arange(n)
    nochange=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       improved=False
       for p in range(n-1):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          ok,nm=pair_update(cur_h,n,cur_c,p,p+1,cur_cmax,idx)
          if ok:
              cur_cmax=nm; improved=True
              if nm<cur_cmax? already
       if cur_cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
           best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_c=cur_c.copy(); best_cmax=cur_cmax
           nochange=0
       else:
           nochange +=1
       if nochange>2: break
    return best_h,best_c,best_cmax
```

```
def pair_update(h,n,c,p,q,max_c,idx):
    if p==q: return False,max_c
    xp=h[p]; yq=h[q]; s=xp+yq
    lo=max(0.0,s-1.0); hi=min(1.0,s)
    if hi-lo<1e-14: return False,max_c
    L=2*n-1; ctr=n-1
    D0=np.zeros(L); D1=np.zeros(L); D2=np.zeros(L); T=np.zeros(L,bool)
    D0[ctr]=-xp*(1-xp)+s*(1-s)-yq*(1-yq); D1[ctr]=2*s; D2[ctr]=-2; T[ctr]=True
    m=ctr+q-p; old=yq*(1-xp); D0[m]+=s-old; D1[m]+=-(s+1); D2[m]+=1; T[m]=True
    m=ctr+p-q; old=xp*(1-yq); D0[m]+=-old; D1[m]+=1-s; D2[m]+=1; T[m]=True
    for r,sgn,oth in ((p,1.0,q),(q,-1.0,p)):
        j=idx[(idx!=r)&(idx!=oth)]
        if j.size:
            m=ctr+j-r; co=h[j]; D1[m]-=co; D0[m]+=xp*co; T[m]=True
            m=ctr+r-j; co=1-h[j]; inc=sgn*co; D1[m]+=inc; D0[m]-=xp*inc; T[m]=True
    if not T.any(): return False,max_c
    unaff = -1e100 if T.all() else float(c[~T].max())
    im=T.nonzero()[0]; cm=c[im]; d0=D0[im]; d1=D1[im]; d2=D2[im]
    xs=np.linspace(lo,hi,256)
    f=pair_eval_idx(cm,d0,d1,d2,unaff,xs)
    i=int(f.argmin())
    if f[i] >= max_c - 1e-10: return False,max_c
    lo2=xs[max(0,i-1)]; hi2=xs[min(255,i+1)]
    for _ in range(2):
        xs=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,400)
        f=pair_eval_idx(...)
        j=int(f.argmin())
        lo2=xs[max(0,j-1)]; hi2=xs[min(399,j+1)]
    x=float(xs[j])
    if x<lo: x=lo
    elif x>hi: x=hi
    new=float(np.max(c+D0+D1*x+D2*x*x))
    if new < max_c - 1e-10:
       h[p]=x; h[q]=s-x; c += D0 + D1*x + D2*x*x
       return True,new
    return False,max_c
```
Need after first refine loop if `f` not defined? yes. If no refinement? loop 2.
Use `min(255,i+1)` because coarse length 256. In refine, length 400, min(399,j+1). Good.
The variable `j` from loop available after loop. Good.
`pair_eval_idx`:
```
def pair_eval_idx(cm,d0,d1,d2,unaff,xs):
    x=xs.reshape(-1,1)
    f=(cm[None,:]+d0[None,:]+d1[None,:]*x+d2[None,:]*x*x).max(axis=1)
    if unaff > -1e90: np.maximum(f,unaff,out=f)
    return f
```
Maybe `x*x` computed twice? okay.

Potential issue: if `lo2,hi2` equal due coarse min at endpoint and i±? If i=0, lo=xs0, hi=xs1, not equal unless domain zero. Good.

Now, pair_cd inline target:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,rng):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); bm=cmax
    ch=h.copy(); cc=c.copy(); cm=cmax
    idx=np.arange(n); L=2*n-1; ctr=n-1
    active=np.argsort(cc)[-max(8,L//25):]; it=0; stall=0; state=0
    top_lags = max(8, L//25)
    if not np.any(active==ctr): active=np.append(active,ctr)
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if it % 300 == 0:
          active=np.argsort(cc)[-top_lags:]
          if not np.any(active==ctr): active=np.append(active,ctr)
       u=rng.random()
       if u<0.45:
          m=int(active[rng.randint(len(active))]); d=m-ctr
          if d==0:
             sc=ch*(1-ch); idxs=np.argsort(sc)[-4:]; p=int(idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))]); q=(p+1)%n
          elif d>0:
             cont=ch[d:]*(1-ch[:-d]); idxs=np.argsort(cont)[-4:]; k=int(idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))]); p=k; q=k+d
          else:
             D=-d; cont=ch[:-D]*(1-ch[D:]); idxs=np.argsort(cont)[-4:]; k=int(idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))]); p=D+k; q=k
       elif u<0.75:
          p=state%(n-1); q=p+1; state+=1
       else:
          p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
          if q==p: q=(p+1)%n
       ok,nm=pair_update(ch,n,cc,p,q,cm,idx)
       if ok:
          cm=nm
          if cm<bm-1e-12:
             bm=cm; bh=ch.copy(); bc=cc.copy()
          stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       it+=1
       if it % 2000 == 0:
          cc2,cm2=compute_c(ch,n); cc,cm=cc2,cm2
          if cm<bm-1e-12: bm=cm; bh=ch.copy(); bc=cc.copy()
          if cm > bm + 0.01:
             ch=bh.copy(); cc=bc.copy(); cm=bm
       if stall > 2500:
          sigma=0.012 + 0.01*(stall%5)
          ch=project_feasible(bh + rng.normal(0,sigma,n),n)
          cc,cm=compute_c(ch,n)
          if cm<bm-1e-12: bm=cm; bh=ch.copy(); bc=cc.copy()
          elif cm > bm + 0.02:
             ch=bh.copy(); cc=bc.copy(); cm=bm
          active=np.argsort(cc)[-top_lags:]
          if not np.any(active==ctr): active=np.append(active,ctr)
          stall=0
    return bh,bc,bm
```
Need `bh` variable defined; use best_h instead. In code, after recompute if cm<bm update best_h/c. Use `best_h` not concise. We'll write clear.
Potential bug: In `elif d>0`, `cont` length n-d; if d >= n? d in [-(n-1),n-1], so if d>0 length >=1. Good. d=0 handled. For d<0 D=-d <=n-1, `cont=ch[:-D]*(1-ch[D:])` length n-D >=1.
`p=(p+1)%n` for n=1? n>1. Good.
`state%(n-1)`: if n-1=1 ok. If n=1 error; not.

Polish adjacent:
```
def polish_adjacent(h,n,c,cmax,deadline,idx):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); bm=cmax
    ch=h.copy(); cc=c.copy(); cm=cmax
    no=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       imp=False
       for p in range(n-1):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          ok,nm=pair_update(ch,n,cc,p,p+1,cm,idx)
          if ok:
             cm=nm; imp=True
             if cm<bm-1e-12: bm=cm; best_h=ch.copy(); best_c=cc.copy()
       if not imp: no+=1
       else: no=0
       if no>=2: break
    return best_h,best_c,bm
```
This updates best only when cm<b. Good. If cm improves but not below best? fine.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
    except Exception:
       n=500; h=np.zeros(n); perm=rng.permutation(n); h[perm[:n//2]]=1.0
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c,cmax=compute_c(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_n=n; bestC=cmax*2.0/n
    reserve=180 if budget_s>600 else 60
    md=deadline-reserve
    if md<=time.time(): md=deadline
    best_h,best_c,bmax=pair_cd(best_h,best_n,best_c,best_cmax,md,rng)
    C2=bmax*2.0/best_n
    if C2<bestC: update
    # multi attempt if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
       for nn in (600,800,500):
          if time.time() > deadline - 70: break
          if nn==best_n: continue
          sub=min(deadline-50,time.time()+80)
          if sub<=time.time(): continue
          try:
             xo=np.linspace(0,2,best_n); xn=np.linspace(0,2,nn)
             hi=project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,best_h),nn)
             ci,cim=compute_c(hi,nn)
             hi,ci,cim=pair_cd(hi,nn,ci,cim,sub,rng)
             Ci=cim*2.0/nn
             if Ci<bestC: best_h,best_c,best_n,bestC=hi,ci,nn,Ci
          except Exception: pass
    # adjacent polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       idxp=np.arange(best_n)
       h3,c3,m3=polish_adjacent(best_h,best_n,best_c,best_cmax,deadline-10,idxp)
       C3=m3*2.0/best_n
       if C3<bestC: update
    # final tiny shake? if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       nh=project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,0.005,best_n),best_n)
       nc,nm=compute_c(nh,best_n); Nc=nm*2.0/best_n
       if Nc < bestC:
          best_h, best_c, bestC = nh, nc, Nc
          c,cmax=compute_c(best_h,best_n); bestC=cmax*2.0/best_n
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    c,bmax=compute_c(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,bmax*2.0/best_n,best_n
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np

def project_feasible(z, n):
    z = np.clip(np.asarray(z, dtype=float).ravel(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if abs(z.sum() - target) < 1e-14:
        return z
    lo = float(z.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(z.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        th = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(z - th, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = th
        else:
            hi = th
    return np.clip(z - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

def compute_c(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return c, float(np.max(c))

def pair_eval_idx(cm, d0, d1, d2, unaff, xs):
    x = xs.reshape(-1, 1)
    x2 = x * x
    f = (cm[None, :] + d0[None, :] + d1[None, :] * x + d2[None, :] * x2).max(axis=1)
    if unaff > -1e90:
        np.maximum(f, unaff, out=f)
    return f

def pair_update(h, n, c, p, q, max_c, idx):
    if p == q:
        return False, max_c
    xp = h[p]
    yq = h[q]
    s = xp + yq
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
        return False, max_c

    L = 2 * n - 1
    ctr = n - 1
    D0 = np.zeros(L)
    D1 = np.zeros(L)
    D2 = np.zeros(L)
    T = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)

    D0[ctr] = -xp * (1.0 - xp) + s * (1.0 - s) - yq * (1.0 - yq)
    D1[ctr] = 2.0 * s
    D2[ctr] = -2.0
    T[ctr] = True

    m = ctr + q - p
    old = yq * (1.0 - xp)
    D0[m] += s - old
    D1[m] += -(s + 1.0)
    D2[m] += 1.0
    T[m] = True

    m = ctr + p - q
    old = xp * (1.0 - yq)
    D0[m] += -old
    D1[m] += 1.0 - s
    D2[m] += 1.0
    T[m] = True

    for r, sgn, oth in ((p, 1.0, q), (q, -1.0, p)):
        j = idx[(idx != r) & (idx != oth)]
        if j.size:
            m = ctr + j - r
            co = h[j]
            D1[m] -= co
            D0[m] += xp * co
            T[m] = True

            m = ctr + r - j
            co = 1.0 - h[j]
            inc = sgn * co
            D1[m] += inc
            D0[m] -= xp * inc
            T[m] = True

    if not T.any():
        return False, max_c

    unaff = -1e100 if T.all() else float(c[~T].max())
    im = T.nonzero()[0]
    cm = c[im]
    d0 = D0[im]
    d1 = D1[im]
    d2 = D2[im]

    xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, 256)
    f = pair_eval_idx(cm, d0, d1, d2, unaff, xs)
    i = int(f.argmin())
    if f[i] >= max_c - 1e-10:
        return False, max_c

    lo2 = xs[max(0, i - 1)]
    hi2 = xs[min(255, i + 1)]
    jbest = i
    for _ in range(2):
        xs = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 400)
        f = pair_eval_idx(cm, d0, d1, d2, unaff, xs)
        j = int(f.argmin())
        lo2 = xs[max(0, j - 1)]
        hi2 = xs[min(399, j + 1)]
        jbest = j

    x = float(xs[jbest])
    if x < lo:
        x = lo
    elif x > hi:
        x = hi

    newc = float(np.max(c + D0 + D1 * x + D2 * x * x))
    if newc < max_c - 1e-10:
        h[p] = x
        h[q] = s - x
        c += D0 + D1 * x + D2 * x * x
        return True, newc
    return False, max_c

def pair_cd(h, n, c, cmax, deadline, rng):
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_cmax = cmax
    ch = h.copy()
    cc = c.copy()
    cm = cmax

    idx = np.arange(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    ctr = n - 1
    top_lags = max(8, L // 25)
    active = np.argsort(cc)[-top_lags:]
    if not np.any(active == ctr):
        active = np.append(active, ctr)

    it = 0
    stall = 0
    state = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if it % 300 == 0:
            active = np.argsort(cc)[-top_lags:]
            if not np.any(active == ctr):
                active = np.append(active, ctr)

        u = rng.random()
        if u < 0.45 and len(active):
            m = int(active[rng.randint(len(active))])
            d = m - ctr
            if d == 0:
                sc = ch * (1.0 - ch)
                kk = np.argsort(sc)[-4:]
                p = int(kk[rng.randint(len(kk))])
                q = (p + 1) % n
            elif d > 0:
                cont = ch[d:] * (1.0 - ch[:-d])
                kk = np.argsort(cont)[-4:]
                k = int(kk[rng.randint(len(kk))])
                p = k
                q = k + d
            else:
                D = -d
                cont = ch[:-D] * (1.0 - ch[D:])
                kk = np.argsort(cont)[-4:]
                k = int(kk[rng.randint(len(kk))])
                p = D + k
                q = k
        elif u < 0.75:
            p = state % (n - 1)
            q = p + 1
            state += 1
        else:
            p = rng.randint(n)
            q = rng.randint(n)
            if q == p:
                q = (p + 1) % n

        ok, nm = pair_update(ch, n, cc, p, q, cm, idx)
        if ok:
            cm = nm
            if cm < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                best_cmax = cm
                best_h = ch.copy()
                best_c = cc.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            cc2, cm2 = compute_c(ch, n)
            cc, cm = cc2, cm2
            if cm < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                best_cmax = cm
                best_h = ch.copy()
                best_c = cc.copy()
            if cm > best_cmax + 0.01:
                ch = best_h.copy()
                cc = best_c.copy()
                cm = best_cmax

        if stall > 2500:
            sigma = 0.01 + 0.005 * (stall % 7)
            ch = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), n)
            cc, cm = compute_c(ch, n)
            if cm < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                best_cmax = cm
                best_h = ch.copy()
                best_c = cc.copy()
            elif cm > best_cmax + 0.02:
                ch = best_h.copy()
                cc = best_c.copy()
                cm = best_cmax
            active = np.argsort(cc)[-top_lags:]
            if not np.any(active == ctr):
                active = np.append(active, ctr)
            stall = 0

    return best_h, best_c, best_cmax

def polish_adjacent(h, n, c, cmax, deadline, idx):
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_cmax = cmax
    ch = h.copy()
    cc = c.copy()
    cm = cmax
    stuck = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        improved = False
        for p in range(n - 1):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            ok, nm = pair_update(ch, n, cc, p, p + 1, cm, idx)
            if ok:
                cm = nm
                improved = True
                if cm < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                    best_cmax = cm
                    best_h = ch.copy()
                    best_c = cc.copy()
        if not improved:
            stuck += 1
        else:
            stuck = 0
        if stuck >= 2:
            break
    return best_h, best_c, best_cmax

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 5
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        n = len(h)
    except Exception:
        n = 500
        h = np.zeros(n)
        perm = rng.permutation(n)
        h[perm[:n // 2]] = 1.0

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c, cmax = compute_c(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_n = n
    bestC = cmax * (2.0 / n)

    idx_main = np.arange(n)

    reserve = 180 if budget_s > 600 else 60
    main_deadline = deadline - reserve
    if main_deadline <= time.time():
        main_deadline = deadline

    best_h, best_c, bmax = pair_cd(best_h, best_n, best_c, bmax, main_deadline, rng)
    C = bmax * (2.0 / best_n)
    if C < bestC:
        bestC = C

    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
        ht = project_feasible((best_h > 0.5).astype(float), best_n)
        ct, tmax = compute_c(ht, best_n)
        tC = tmax * (2.0 / best_n)
        if tC < bestC + 0.03:
            sub = min(deadline - 70, time.time() + 80)
            if sub > time.time():
                h3, c3, m3 = pair_cd(ht, best_n, ct, tmax, sub, rng)
                C3 = m3 * (2.0 / best_n)
                if C3 < bestC:
                    best_h = h3.copy()
                    best_c = c3.copy()
                    bestC = C3

    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
        idx_pol = np.arange(best_n)
        cc = best_c.copy()
        cm = float(np.max(cc))
        h3, c3, m3 = polish_adjacent(best_h, best_n, cc, cm, deadline - 8, idx_pol)
        C3 = m3 * (2.0 / best_n)
        if C3 < bestC:
            best_h = h3.copy()
            best_c = c3.copy()
            bestC = C3

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    _, bmax = compute_c(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, bmax * (2.0 / best_n), best_n
```
